## PART A — L10, THE EDITOR (verbatim; the same text stands in the compiled volume's §1)

**L10 — THE EDITOR.** Edit Mode and the Decree Registry landed on the build branch between 2026-09-19 and 2026-09-23 (docs/DESIGN_EDIT_MODE_AND_DECREES.md §13–§22 and docs/ARCH_EDIT_MODE_AND_DECREES.md are the design and the build contract; docs/implementation/surveys/SIM-SEALS-SURVEY-2026-09-19.md is the measured map of every simulation option a DM could direct; the Edit Mode charter docs/implementation/charters/EDIT-MODE-TRAIN.md carries the members). The editor is ONE door into ONE queue applied at ONE moment: the DM edits FACTS from POOLS, every change is a typed OP through the one mutation boundary, a canonized town takes its changes as DECREES applied at the head of the tick as first-class causes, GUARDS inform and never refuse, a process card wears a SEAL (a directive — a `pin-fork` over a registered fork's own outcome vocabulary, or a DIRECTION over a layer's own action), an off-stage counterparty is a PHANTOM that can absorb an act and never return one, and entity states (an NPC's `status`, an institution's `state`) are typed roots with one writer each. Spine requirement 14 (the Edit-verb story) was written against the Change Dock and the operationRegistry lane; the editor REPLACED the Change Dock, so requirement 14's MECHANICS are superseded by this law and its TOTALITY survives verbatim: a state with no verb is a RECORDED decision ("engine-only, because X"), never silence. Every remaining FP wave carries an EDITOR line with five members (§12 carries the lines; a landed wave's old Edit-verb clause is history):

(a) **FORKS.** Every stochastic choice the wave mints — a `w * hash01` race, a keyed roll, a softmax over candidates — registers its row in `HABIT_FORK_REGISTRY` (`src/domain/worldPulse/habitForkRegistry.js`) IN THE SAME COMMIT, with its `actionVocabulary` declared when the outcome is a closed word set (so `pin-fork` can direct it the day it exists) or classed STAY / derivation-only WITH THE REASON; the chooser-totality walker's figures are re-measured by execution, never inherited. The survey's three dangerous classes are never pinnable and are declared as such where they occur: a receipt-prose pick (it would pin the herald's sentence, not the world), an observation's noise (the world would lie with provenance reading `observed`), and a verdict's band without its roll (a pinned verdict carries its band — §19 ruling 3). Extends L1: the hash key stays the stream identity; the registry row names it. A wave whose fork has no symbol (the `piety` class) registers nothing and says so.

(b) **SEALS AND CONDITIONS.** A wave that mints a PROCESS a DM could direct — a pact proposal, a mission, a tithe, a pilgrimage, an omen read, a schism, a venture, a corner, a seizure, a call-in, a relay, a fission, a renewal, a mediation — lands in the same wave: (i) its §18 WORLD-STATE PREDICATE in `src/domain/edit/worldConditions.js` by symbol, read from the record and the campaign and TRUE in a real world (a writer reaches it from `src/`, measured — never a predicate that is "absent until" a later wave; that is the standing-offer defect, U105); (ii) the ACT the seal binds, which is the layer's OWN action, found by THIS LADDER and never minted before the ladder is exhausted: a realm-manifest verb where one exists (`src/domain/events/realmManifest.js :: REALM_MANIFEST`, its own predicate as the condition and `realmVetoProse(code, detail)` as the herald's reason); else a declared direction where one exists (`src/domain/edit/directions.js :: DIRECTION_OP_TYPES` — the force, the muster, the levy, the occupier's posture, the tempo, the brokerage menu, the channel status, the three belief ops, the credibility kinds); else a registered fork's pin (`HABIT_FORK_REGISTRY`, a row WITH `actionVocabulary`); else an off-stage op (`operationsOffStage.js :: OFF_STAGE_OP_TYPES`); else a NEW direction row DEFINED in the wave's own module in `operations.js`'s `OpTypeDeclaration` shape with its vocabulary imported from the module that owns it (`directions.js`'s law: not one word of vocabulary is spelled in the registry) and imported by the registry at the merge — the chair's composition. The same holds for a NEW world-state predicate: defined in the owning module in `worldConditions.js`'s `liveRow` shape and composed into `WORLD_CONDITIONS` by the chair at the merge, the partition arm re-recorded with the cause. Every act routes through `changeAuthorityPolicy.js :: authorityFor` / `actorMajorApproval.js :: ACTOR_INITIATED_MAJOR_TYPES`; a `pin-fork` or a direction that bypasses the approval queue is a second write path and is refused at compile; (iii) its seal row on the card's per-card table with the herald's reason line for the missing condition; or (iv) the record "no seal, by design" with the survey's Table 2 class. A seal is never a wall: the missing condition names the act that creates it ("No peace has been offered. Make them sue for it, or wait."). The phantom column is law (§19 ruling 10): a two-sided act is `real-only`; a home act is `home+record`.

(c) **THE COUNTERPARTY.** Every inter-settlement mechanism the wave builds declares its phantom policy AT THE SEAM: `real-only` (a treaty, a pact, a route, a contest, a coalition, a channel, a war state — §13: nothing of these is ever derived from a phantom; not offered on one, and the card says why) or `home+record` (the home procedures act on the home's own state; the chronicle carries the declaration or the outcome, marked off-stage). Every reader of neighbour rows the wave adds accepts a phantom partner — a minimal save record, `kind: 'phantom'`, no simulated side — without throwing: one pin per reader. Promotion never rewrites the past: a phantom's record-only outcomes stay record-only after it is forged (THE PROMISE).

(d) **THE TICK AND THE STATES.** Decrees apply at the HEAD of the tick as first-class causes (`applyDecreesAtTick`, the one hook `pulseKernel.js` gained — L1's zero-edit law on the kernel stands: FP mounts nothing there), before every FP stage; an FP stage reads the post-decree world and never caches a fact across the head. An FP stage that CASTS a person — an envoy, a spy, a factor, a pilgrim, a legate, a mediator — reads the ONE participation chokepoint, `src/domain/roads/state.js :: isOffStage` over `OFF_STAGE_STATUSES` (derived from `src/domain/entities/npcs.js :: NPC_UNAVAILABLE_STATUSES`; `dead` is paired by each consumer; `missing` and `retired` are DELIBERATELY absent, per that module's own words — a wave cites the symbols and never restates the list), and never a filter of its own; away-on-mission is the errand record's position, read from it, never a status. An FP stage that moves an institution's standing writes through the writer the `set-institution-state` op uses (`calamityKernel.js :: ruinInstitution` for ruin), never a parallel state; an FP stage that moves a person out of play writes through the writers the `set-npc-status` op uses (the ops layer's existing writers — no single exported NPC-status symbol exists at the base; the first wave that needs one names it and lands it once). An applied decree's chronicle line comes from the decree pools with `cause: 'table' | 'party'` — L6's five joins gain a sixth for any FP news kind a decree can cause: the cause vocabulary row. Pins, directions and off-stage acts reopen READ-ONLY once applied; the rewind is `undoLastPulse` and retracts the tick's lines (THE PROMISE, §12.1 of the design).

(e) **THE RECORD AND THE VOCABULARIES.** An FP wave that writes a first-hand fact on the SETTLEMENT RECORD (a `creedRef`, an appetite, a house's standing, a doctrine sheet) classes it BY PATH in the record register (`src/domain/edit/recordRegister.js`: HELD · WORLD · CONSTANT · READING · MIRROR · RECEIPT · HISTORY · AUTHORED) in the same commit — the totality walker reds an unclassed key — and a HELD fact is either a ROOT (a field declaration with its pool, on the card's table) or wears a provenance note. worldState-side state — the fourteen `spatialLedgers` sub-keys of §4, the conditional fields on pulse records — is LIVED HISTORY outside the record register and outside `rederive`: FP never adds to the held set generation re-derives, and this clause is the statement of it. A vocabulary a decree can point into (an op type, a fork id, an outcome word, a pool value, a catalogue event, a mission kind) is SAVE-DATA CONTRACT: renaming or removing a member is a vocabulary move that §20.3's resolver withdraws pending decrees on with a reason — the wave names the move and lands the arm that proves the withdrawal, never a silent rename.

**Precedence.** Where this law and the Edit Mode design disagree on the EDITOR'S MODEL, the design governs and the disagreement is a bug to report; where they disagree on FP SUBSTANCE (what a treaty, a mission, a tithe IS), the FP volumes govern. Provenance: the seal survey (146 candidate rows; at the survey 1 of 42 registry rows carried a vocabulary, at this fold 6 of 86; the owner's named acts were unregistered draws), the cure day's measurement that twelve of the shell's sixteen seals name an act no catalogue can express and one bound seal could never light in a real world (U104, U105), and the standing-offer STOP (E4e v1) — each a defect this law prevents at the wave, not at the surface.

## PART B — THE CHAIR'S RULINGS OVER THE DRAFTS

The three per-family tables below were drafted by measurement seats under L10 and are reproduced VERBATIM; every ⚖ cell in them is a proposal until it appears here as a ruling. Where a table and this section disagree, THIS SECTION GOVERNS. Where a ruling and the tree disagree at build, the builder STOPS with the measured contradiction.

**WF · POP · INT · WY (the draft's J1–J23).** R-1 J1–J2, J4–J7, J9–J11, J13–J21, J23 ACCEPTED as drafted. R-2 (J3 amended): the inbound-envoy reader is **SP-D2's** (§12.5, lane FP-A), over every purpose class; WF-2b's legates RIDE it and supply the religious sender, never a second reader. R-3 (J8 ruled): there is ONE tithe — WF-7's temple tithe IS the treasury's `tithe_share` levy form (`treasury.js :: TAX_FORMS`), `templeWealth` accrues from it, and no second stock or form is minted; "Remit the tithe." binds `set-levy`. R-4 (J12 ruled): rumour weathering (WY-2's per-hop roll, WY-5's mover hop) is NEVER PINNABLE — transmission noise, the survey's D-2 class — and the survey's row 108 candidacy is struck; the rows still REGISTER (L10 (a)) with their outcome words and the class stated. R-5 (J22 ruled): the shell's `directTrade` seal is ONE DOOR WITH SEVERAL ACTS — the trade card's pop-up offers the channel status and the brokerage menu (EM-E5's rows), the caravan (WY-3's dispatch), the WHERE composer (TR-3's `direct-trade`), grain (TR-4), the pact (TR-5 through GR-2b), the venture (TR-7) as each lands; no act displaces another. R-6: `institutions[].creedRef` (WF-5) is class HELD with a provenance note, never a pooled root (J6), and it is the ONLY settlement-record key this family writes.

**ES · HB · WC · EP (the draft's cross-cutting 1–5 and per-row ⚖).** R-7 (cross-cutting 1, ruled as law): **THE TABLE'S HAND TEACHES NOTHING** — a pinned or directed choice opens no habit pledge, grades no close, and the habit and anticipation loads skip a pinned tick; otherwise decrees would train the courts through a second write path. R-8 (2): ES-7's `DISPATCH_CONFIRMATION_MISSION` is SUPERSEDED by `send-on-mission`; the WC volume §7.D's seven verbs split as drafted — three annotations become the DM layer's `free` fields (`annotateContributionRow`, `editBlockRosterNote`, `annotateCohort`), four become acts through `authorityFor` (`forgiveContributionDebt` an op, `removeFreeUnit` an op with the two dispositions, `resolveEmbattledState` the brigand pin, `setVeteranPolicy` a PIN — never a HELD root on the record for a campaign-side policy). R-9 (3): WC-4 binds the deferred realm verb `REINFORCE` to `contributionDispatch` and lifts its deferral; WC-6 supplies the column seam `REINFORCE`/`INTERCEPT` wait on; no second "send aid" surface. R-10 (4): people never shed into a phantom; WC's conservation identity is total across §13's phantom wars using home-side events only (`dispatch`, `fell`, the sink, `arrive_home`). R-11 (5): ES-7's six kinds compose onto W-OPS `operationsVoice.js` (`MISSION_BEAT_KINDS`, `EXPOSURE_MOMENTS`), never a parallel espionage voice. R-12 (ES-2b): the covert hold uses THE SAME road WR-7b's diplomatic hold uses to take a person off stage (whatever that road sets — the builder names it by symbol); one chokepoint, one reading; the gauntlet header's `openEnvoyInterception` (no definition at the base) is re-addressed to the gate the builder measures. R-13 (ES-6b): EM-E7's direction casts through `castCovertOperative` via ES-Db — that is what unblocks ES-6b; the two-word vetting threshold is a tuning value on the owner's signature surface. R-14 (HB-4/HB-5/HB-9): a fork site is registered when the four-idiom scan finds a draw and classed STAY with reason when it is a threshold; the peace answer's {accept, refuse} pair is MINTED where the deciding module owns it, never borrowed from `BALLOT_DECISIONS` (different facts); `occupation.js :: STATE_LADDER` is EXPORTED by HB-9. R-15 (WC-5): the call-in request record lives on WC's ledger (worldState), never on the settlement record; `lapsed` is silence past the horizon and stays unpinnable. R-16 (WC-8): a pin applies to the WHOLE block. R-17 (EP-4): Req 14 is recorded as generator-only surfaces; H1's removal half is retired (J-EM-6); `_seed` stays class WORLD and `rederive` never mints. R-18 (EP-5): the amended promise gains the decree clause — "a pinned outcome survives a re-advance; the unpinned world lives a new future" — and the home list is re-derived at EP-5's compile (the editor added fifteen promise lines across seven files).

**SP · CW · GR · IN · TR (the draft's ⚖ and its six disagreements).** R-19 (the six disagreements): all six ACCEPTED — L10 (b)(ii) now carries the full ladder including EM-E5's declared directions, names `realmVetoProse(code, detail)`, and (d) cites `isOffStage`/`OFF_STAGE_STATUSES` by symbol with `missing` and `retired` deliberately absent; the "absent until" rule stands as the law FP obeys even though the editor's own `envoyArrived` broke it (SP-D2 cures that row); the NPC-status writer is named by the first wave that lands one. R-20 (GR-1): the remaining stamp's pick reads `isOffStage`; the LANDED stamp doors are NOT retrofitted in this program — their retrofit is a declared golden shift, one cause, through the owner's signed door at the soak; the extraction leaf is `generosityObligationStamp.js` beside `generosityKernel.js` (the kernel's ceiling untouched). R-21 (GR-2b): a `REALM_MANIFEST` row (J-EM-11); the host's answer to a proposal made TO the DM's town IS the DM's act (the DM plays the host court) — a direction over `PACT_PROPOSAL_STATES` 'signed'/'refused' through `authorityFor`; `PROPOSE_PACT` takes the proposal lane like every realm verb (candidateType `pact_proposed`; whether it joins `ACTOR_INITIATED_MAJOR_TYPES` is measured against `changeAuthorityPolicy.js`'s own rules at build, never assumed). R-22 (GR-4b-β): ONE VOICE PER ACT — the repudiated line renders only when no `treaty_breached` beat fired for the same act. R-23 (GR-4b-ii): parked, the owner's (a persisted key). R-24 (GR-5b..5d): the bands are chair-signed at each compile from measurement; a DM may open a renewal through GR-2b; `renegotiation` joins `PACT_TRIGGERS` (growth); conversion stays the COURT'S — the DM's lever is the label through `set-relationship`. R-25 (GR-6): "Offer good offices." is a DIRECTION through `authorityFor`, never a mission; it can only add pressure (the charter's never-forces pin). R-26 (GR-7, IN-6, TR-9, CW-3, WF-9, POP-7): every measurement instrument counts decree-caused acts APART, by `DECREE_CAUSE`, and declares its directed-world arm separately — a DM's decrees never satisfy an envelope or a floor. R-27 (IN-1c-b): the survey's row 105 is reclassed derivation-only; the interim desk stays `section: null` until IN-5. R-28 (IN-2): the lie commission is a DIRECTION over the plant road with an axis-typed subject, never a widening of `mutate-belief`'s payload; `rumourReinforcesAt` registers here (J-EM-5). R-29 (IN-3): the SWEEP binds `castDoubt`'s world side; VET is a third reception arm, "Test their word.", beside receive and turn-away. R-30 (IN-4): the survey's row 41 is reclassed no-seal (arrival physics); a courier kind joins `MISSION_KINDS` (additive). R-31 (IN-5): section tokens are not decree-addressable. R-32 (TR-2): house acts are DM-directable as ONE direction, "Direct the house.", over the closed verb set; formation, the ruin latch and rename stay latches; `genesisPatronOf` registers here (J-EM-5). R-33 (TR-3): `directTrade` binds the WHERE composer as `direct-trade`, beside EM-E5's channel status (R-5). R-34 (TR-5): the pact arm rides GR-2b's verb with trigger `trade_demand`; the count is THREE (J-EM-6). R-35 (TR-6): a corner is DM-directable and pays the moral price; alignment never gates it. R-36 (TR-8): the module is `factorErrand.js` — the registry's spelling (`ERRAND_CONSUMERS`) wins over the charter's `commercialErrands`. R-37 (CW-1, CW-2x): an applied decree is a TRUE ROOT of a chain and renders with the table as its ancestor; a phantom's record-only outcome is a braid member, never a live ancestor.

**Cross-family.** R-38: every predicate a wave lands returns SUBJECTS (J-EM-3) — a row without subjects is a row for no seal. R-39: every "no seal, by design" cell in the tables is the wave's Req 14 record (the totality of spine requirement 14 survives as those cells). R-40: the tables' "to mint" vocabularies are minted where the deciding module lives, additive, and each becomes save-data contract the day a decree can name it (L10 (e)).

## PART C — THE PER-WAVE EDITOR LINES (three family tables, verbatim from the measurement seats; ⚖ = ruled in PART B)

### PART C · family SP-CW-GR-IN-TR

# FP EDITOR LINES — family SP · CW · GR · IN · TR (draft, second pass; read-only)

**Base:** `8b5565922`. Every symbol cited is present at the base: it was read in the `fp-read` worktree or checked with `git grep`, never taken from the stale main checkout. **Law:** `briefs/DRAFT-L10-THE-EDITOR.md`, members (a) to (e). **Scope:** every PARTIAL or NOT STARTED wave in this family (see `FP-CENSUS-SP-CW-GR-IN-TR.md`). A partial wave appears here as its remainder slices only. SP-A..E, CW-0w, GR-0, GR-3, IN-0 and TR-1 have landed and carry no line.

**Marks.** ▪ = derived straight from the charter (the §5 block, the per-program volume `DESIGN_FP_ARCH_xx.md`, the design volume `DESIGN_FP_xx.md`, or the slice's packet). ⚖ = a CHOICE, which is the chair's to make. A name followed by "(to mint)" does not exist at the base, and no vocabulary word is invented here. "live" means a writer in `src/` reaches the state today; "lit" qualifies a state that is written only while a flag is on.

**What the base already holds, which the law's ladder in (b)(ii) should name.**
- The realm manifest: `REALM_MANIFEST`, with herald reasons through `realmVetoProse`.
- Registered forks: `HABIT_FORK_REGISTRY` has **86 rows at the base**. Six declare an `actionVocabulary`: HBF-05, 17, 69, 84, 85 and 86.
- The seven off-stage ops: `operationsOffStage.js :: OFF_STAGE_OP_TYPES`.
- EM-E5's **declared directions**: `directions.js :: DIRECTION_OP_TYPES` holds `correct-belief`, `direct-credibility`, `direct-force`, `direct-realm`, `mutate-belief`, `offer-brokerage`, `set-belief-confidence`, `set-channel-status`, `set-levy`, `set-muster`, `set-occupation-posture` and `set-tempo`. These are NOT composed into `OP_TYPES`; that composition is "the chair's, at the unfreeze".
- The one bound seal: `editSlice.js :: SEAL_ACTS` has one row, `acceptPeace` → `make-peace`. The other fifteen shell seals are shut.

## The seven shut seals — the act behind each, and which wave supplies it

| seal (shell card) | §18 condition at the base | act at the base | FP wave that supplies the act or predicate | still owed outside FP |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **receiveEnvoy** / **turnEnvoyAway** (trade) | `envoyArrived` = `ABSENT_UNTIL_E4`: it answers false in every world, so the seal can never light | none bound | **GR-2b** for pact envoys (the answer, as a direction over `PACT_PROPOSAL_STATES` 'signed' / 'refused'). **TR-5** and **TR-8** for commercial envoys and factors. **IN-3**'s VET adds a third arm ⚖ | EM-E4's inbound-envoy read over the SENDER's errand (`envoyErrandRecords.js :: envoyErrandsOf`, per design §19 ruling 7). War envoys are WR-7's parlay (`negotiateEnvoyParlay`), not FP |
| **directTrade** (trade) | `openRoute` (live) | none bound; EM-E5's `set-channel-status` exists | **TR-3**: §16's `direct-trade` over the WHERE composer ⚖. **TR-4** (grain), **TR-5** (pacts) and **TR-7** (ventures) extend it | EM-E4d's ruling on which verb the herald phrase means |
| **embargo** (trade) | `tradeWith` (live) | **BUILT**: `REALM_MANIFEST.DECLARE_TRADE_EMBARGO`, reached through `direct-realm`, with the off-stage `close-trade` for a phantom | **no remaining FP wave**. TR-1 (landed) supplies the herald's "why" (`commercialReasonTaxonomy.js :: SEVERANCE_REASON_TYPES`) | the binding ruling. The verb's own gate is `warLayerEnabled` AND `supplyWebWarfareEnabled` |
| **confirmRumour** (rumour) | `beliefExists` (live) | DECLARED: `correct-belief` (not composed, not in `SEAL_ACTS`) | **none needed for the act**. IN-6's banded truth provider gives the DM preview ⚖, and IN-5's desk carries the line | EM's binding at the unfreeze |
| **castDoubt** (rumour) | `beliefExists` (live) | DECLARED: `set-belief-confidence`, whose payload is a free 0..1 with no band vocabulary (its own `guardsStated` says so) | **IN-3** supplies the world-side cause (`suspicionOf`, the SWEEP) ⚖ | ⚖ a confidence band vocabulary (to mint), or keep the number (`settlementRumors.js :: confidenceBand` is display-only) |
| **twistRumour** (rumour) | `beliefExists` (live) | DECLARED: `mutate-belief`, whose payload is only `BELIEF_STRENGTH_BANDS` | **IN-2**: the axis variants (SP-B's band constants plus `faithLabel`) that §16's "a recorded variant" wants, and the lie commission as the world-side act on a real court | EM's binding |

## Disagreements between the draft law and the tree, reported (L10's precedence: a bug to report, never silently taken)

1. **(d)'s absence set is not the tree's.**
   - L10 lists `exiled, jailed, missing, dead, retired, removed`.
   - The tree's ONE chokepoint is `roads/state.js :: isOffStage` over `OFF_STAGE_STATUSES` = `NPC_UNAVAILABLE_STATUSES` minus `dead`, which is exiled, jailed and removed. Consumers pair `dead` themselves.
   - `missing` and `retired` are *deliberately* absent: per `entities/npcs.js`, "a missing figure may walk back … a retired elder may still be named a successor". `envoyCasting.js` adds `missing` for dispatch only.
   - The law should cite the symbols rather than restate a list.
2. **(b)(ii) names `REALM_VETO_PROSE`.** The tree has the module-private `REALM_VETO_PROSE_LOCAL` and the exported door `realmManifest.js :: realmVetoProse(code, detail)`.
3. **(b)(ii)'s ladder omits EM-E5's declared directions** (above). "Bind to what is built" should list them after the realm verbs; the three rumour seals and `set-channel-status` already have acts there.
4. **(b)(i)'s "never a predicate that is absent until" rule is already broken in the editor itself.** `envoyArrived` is `ABSENT_UNTIL_E4` at the base. That is EM's own U105 case; GR-2b can supply a live predicate for pact envoys, and the general read remains EM-E4's.
5. **(d) names "the one NPC-status writer".** At the base, `operations.js` declares the op `set-npc-status`, which "calls the existing ops-layer writers, never a second path", but grep finds no single exported writer symbol to cite. The institution side has one: `calamityKernel.js :: ruinInstitution` behind `set-institution-state`. The law should name the NPC writer once it lands.
6. **(a)'s provenance** ("1 of 42") is history. At the base the registry holds 86 rows with 6 vocabularies, and the dangerous classes stand: HBF-07 and HBF-33 in this family are D-1.

## Counts

27 lines:
- **15 mint a directable process** (GR-2b, GR-5b, GR-5c, GR-5d, GR-6, IN-2, IN-3, IN-4, TR-2..TR-8).
- **12 are "no seal, by design"** (GR-1 stamp, GR-4b-β, GR-4b-ii remainder, GR-5e/f, GR-7, IN-1c-b, IN-5, IN-6, TR-9 remainder, CW-1, CW-2x, CW-3).
- **Owner or chair gates:** GR-4b-ii's persisted key (owner). GR-4b-β's double-voicing (chair). The GR-5b..5d bands (chair-signed). IN-1c-b, Q3/Q5 (chair). GR-7, IN-6, TR-9, CW-1 and CW-3 (the owner's soak).
- **Casts that must read `isOffStage`:** GR-1 and GR-5 re-stamps, GR-2b envoys, GR-6 (if a person), IN-2 mouthpiece, IN-4 courier, TR-2 factor, TR-6 factor, TR-7 supercargo, TR-8 factor.

### PART C · family WF-POP-INT-WY

# FP EDITOR LINES — family WF · POP · INT · WY-engine (the remaining 32 waves), drafted under DRAFT-L10

- **Base:** `8b5565922`, read-only worktree `<SC>/fp-read`. Drafted 2026-09-23, 23:0x EDT. This is a design draft for the chair: nothing was built, and every cell is vetoable.
- **Marks.** ▪ means derived straight from the wave's compiled block, its program volume's spec, or the L10 text. ⚖ means a CHOICE for the chair; each ⚖ cell carries its recommendation, and the numbered list at the foot repeats them. "to mint" means no constant exists at the base; no vocabulary word below is invented.

## What the base already has (the anchors every row cites)

These facts were measured at `8b5565922`.

- **The shell's seals.** `src/components/edit/EditModeShell.jsx :: SEALS` defines 16 seals. Only ONE has a writer: `src/store/editSlice.js :: SEAL_ACTS` = `{ acceptPeace → 'make-peace', needs 'pendingPeaceOffer' }`. The other fifteen are shut (judgment 296).
- **The world conditions.** `src/domain/edit/worldConditions.js :: WORLD_CONDITIONS` holds 10 ids: `beliefExists`, `envoyArrived`, `forceInField`, `npcPresent`, `openRoute`, `pendingPeaceOffer`, `plotInMotion`, `siegeInProgress`, `tradeWith`, `warInProgress`. `envoyArrived` is `ABSENT_UNTIL_E4`: it answers false with `readers: []`, which is the U105 standing-offer shape.
- **The act surfaces.** `directions.js :: DIRECTION_OP_TYPES` has 12 rows (`direct-force` over `convergence.js :: ENGAGEMENT_MOVES`, `set-levy` over `treasury.js :: TAX_FORMS/TAX_RATE_BANDS`, `set-muster` over `mobilization.js :: RAMP`, `set-tempo` over `TEMPO_TIERS`, the belief trio, `set-channel-status`, `offer-brokerage`, `direct-credibility`, `set-occupation-posture`, `direct-realm`). `operationsOffStage.js :: OFF_STAGE_OP_TYPES` has 7 (`close-trade`, `declare-war`, `make-peace`, `open-trade`, `recall-force`, `resolve-outcome`, `send-force`). `events/realmManifest.js :: REALM_MANIFEST` has 16 verbs, with `realmVetoProse` as the herald's reason. The authority gate is `changeAuthorityPolicy.js :: authorityFor` plus `actorMajorApproval.js :: ACTOR_INITIATED_MAJOR_TYPES`, and pins route through `directives.js :: pinApplyMode`.
- **Forks.** `habitForkRegistry.js :: HABIT_FORK_REGISTRY` has 86 rows, and 6 carry an `actionVocabulary` (`STAY_DETECTION_OUTCOMES`, `STRATEGY_MOVES`, `RETURN_ODDS_WORDS`, `STRESSOR_LIFECYCLE_STAGES`, `TRADITION_OUTCOME`, `SIEGE_VERDICT_BANDS`). `religionState.js :: resolvePatronContest` and `rulingPowerCoup.js :: resolveCoupVerdict` are still UNREGISTERED at base.
- **States.** The participation chokepoint is `roads/state.js :: isOffStage` over `OFF_STAGE_STATUSES` (EM-B1f). The status vocabulary is `entities/npcs.js :: NPC_STATUS_VALUES` (seven). The institution-state writer is the `set-institution-state` op (`operations.js`, pool active/destroyed/impaired/removed/ruined/vacant); the pulse's ruin is `calamityKernel.js :: ruinInstitution`.
- **Decree causes and the record.** The decree cause vocabulary is `display/stateProse/decreeProsePools.js :: DECREE_LINE_CAUSES` = `['party','table']`, and `decreeHook.js :: applyDecreesAtTick` applies them. The record register is `edit/recordRegister.js :: RECORD_CLASSES`. The §20.3 resolver is `edit/registry.js :: resolveDecree` with `RESOLUTION_MISSING_KINDS` = event · fork · op-type · outcome · pool-value · target.
- **The one settlement-record write in this family** is WF-5's `institutions[].creedRef`. Every other persisted addition is worldState-side (`religionStates`, `spatialLedgers.*`, `deployments`, `spatialDigest`), which is LIVED HISTORY outside the register and outside `rederive` (L10 (e)).

## ⚖ judgments for the chair (each vetoable; the recommendation comes first)

- **J1.** WF-0's deity-bearing fixtures reach deities "through SET_PRIMARY_DEITY/IMPOSE_CULT". On a canonized fixture these are DECREES applied at the head; on a draft they are corrections (§2.6). Pin both.
- **J2.** "Send a legate" binds EM-E7's `send-on-mission`, with a religious mission kind to mint, rather than a WF-2-owned direction: one door for every person sent.
- **J3.** WF-2b supplies the `envoyArrived` reader for inbound legates from REAL senders (§19 ruling 7). The shell's two envoy seals wait on that row. It lands only with a writer that reaches it (U105).
- **J4.** Omen readings are pinnable believers' acts, with the lens band carried (the §19 ruling 3 shape). They are not observation noise.
- **J5.** WF-5's split pins wait for `resolvePatronContest` to be registered (unregistered at base). The founding binds the existing `add-institution` op.
- **J6.** `institutions[].creedRef` is HELD, carrying a PROVENANCE note rather than a pool root.
- **J7.** Faith terms ride the peace-draft record's terms sheet; there is no faith-specific seal.
- **J8.** The tithe's DM act binds the existing `set-levy` over `'tithe_share'`. The two-tithes collision (WF-7's temple tithe against the treasury's form) needs a ruling before WF-7 compiles.
- **J9.** WF-8's realm arcs stay derivation-only (survey #141 is not a seal for them).
- **J10.** WF-9 and POP-7 envelopes are measured on undirected worlds, and a directed-world arm is declared.
- **J11.** Phantoms are never migration endpoints, so POP-1, POP-5a and WY-4 are real-only.
- **J12.** Rumour weathering (WY-2, WY-5) is NEVER PINNABLE, because it is transmission noise. This overrides the survey's #108 candidacy.
- **J13.** "Answer the commons" is a new home direction, LIVE-GATED on `commonsVoiceActive`.
- **J14.** POP-2's refusal rung is appended to the rung order, never inserted.
- **J15.** POP-5a's column events become a pinnable fork (vocabulary to mint), a sibling of survey #42.
- **J16.** The population editor reads the `set-tempo` tier; there is one loudness surface.
- **J17.** The émigré host decision binds `ADMISSION_OUTCOMES`. No arrival predicate exists before the record's home is ruled (owner, Q-TC12-1).
- **J18.** An exile toward a phantom is home+record.
- **J19.** The burial verb is a new `REALM_MANIFEST` row, and it is real-only.
- **J20.** INT-7's writer census includes W-SEAT's upheaval lane.
- **J21.** `kmScale` has no seal (engine-only, with the reason recorded).
- **J22.** WY-3's dispatch is the ACT behind the shell's shut `directTrade` seal, and phantom trade stays the existing `open-trade`.
- **J23.** Seizure binds `direct-force` 'engage' rather than minting a pin.

**CONFIRMED vs PLAUSIBLE:** every symbol, vocabulary, op, verb, condition and registry fact cited as existing is CONFIRMED by reads at `8b5565922` (source reads and `git grep`; the registry was read by module import). Every ⚖ cell, and every predicate or act marked "to mint", is a PLAUSIBLE design draft. The forks' draw-versus-derivation calls for WF-2b's dispatch, WF-5's exposure, WF-7's remission, POP-2's answer, INT-3b's trigger and INT-6's autonomous burial are VERIFY-AT-BUILD.

### PART C · family ES-HB-WC-EP

# FP EDITOR LINES (L10) — ES · HB · WC · EP, the remaining waves

- **Base:** `8b5565922`, read-only (`git show` / `git grep`). Nothing run, nothing committed.
- **Rows:** every PARTIAL or NOT STARTED wave from `FP-CENSUS-ES-HB-WC-EP.md`, 28 rows in all:
  - ES-2b and ES-6b (the owed slices of the PARTIAL waves ES-2 and ES-6);
  - ES-7 (refused and parked; ES-6c folds here per the ES volume);
  - HB-3…HB-9 (gated);
  - WC-1…WC-16 (WC-6 onward gated);
  - EP-4 and EP-5.
- **Marks:** ▪ = derived straight from the charter (the compiled block plus the per-program volume) or from a symbol measured at base. ⚖ = a CHOICE for the chair.
- **Vocabulary rule:** every vocabulary is either a constant that exists at base, cited as `path :: symbol`, or written as "to mint". A name written "to mint (`X`)" is the per-program volume's chartered name; it does not exist at base.

## Cross-cutting ⚖ for the chair (one line each)

1. **Learning from the table's hand.** A pinned or directed choice should open **no habit pledge**, and the habit and anticipation loads should be skipped on pinned ticks (HB-3/4/5/7/9). Otherwise decrees train the courts through a second write path.
2. **Superseded Edit-verbs.** ES-7's `DISPATCH_CONFIRMATION_MISSION` becomes `send-on-mission`. The WC volume §7.D's seven DM verbs split: three annotations become the layer's `free` field (`annotateContributionRow`, `editBlockRosterNote`, `annotateCohort`), and four become pins, ops or directions through `authorityFor` (`forgiveContributionDebt`, `removeFreeUnit`, `resolveEmbattledState`, `setVeteranPolicy`). HB-8's "edit-verb wave" becomes provenance with no pencil. EP-4's Req 14 is generator-only. **Req 14's totality survives** as the recorded decision in each row.
3. **Bind what is built.** WC-4/WC-6 supply the seam that the deferred realm verbs `REINFORCE` and `INTERCEPT` wait on. Binding them beats minting a second "send reinforcements" surface.
4. **The phantom and the people ledger.** WC's conservation walker must be total across §13's phantom wars, using home-side events only. People never shed into a phantom.
5. **One voice.** ES-7's six kinds should compose onto W-OPS `operationsVoice.js`, whose `EXPOSURE_MOMENTS` include `taken`, rather than mint a parallel espionage voice.

**CONFIRMED vs PLAUSIBLE.** CONFIRMED by executed read-only greps at `8b5565922`: every cited symbol, vocabulary and "to mint" absence, the registry figures, `SEAL_ACTS`, the `WORLD_CONDITIONS` ids, the `REINFORCE` deferral and the 15 PROMISE lines. The ⚖ cells are PLAUSIBLE by construction: they are proposals for the chair. Whether the WC-10, WC-11, WC-13, WC-14 and WC-16 forks actually draw is chartered in the WC volume, not measured in code, because none of that code exists yet.

## PART D — THE SEAM MEASUREMENT (verbatim; one seat, read-only, base `8b5565922`)

# EM × FP SEAM — MEASUREMENT (one seat; read-only; base `8b5565922`; 2026-09-23 22:5x EDT)

**Brief:** `fp-kit/briefs/MEASURE-EM-FP-SEAM.md`. **Source read:** the read-only worktree `<SC>/fp-read` at `8b55659223246e638448c2cf80ecec4d6a9bea48` (clean), plus `docs/DESIGN_EDIT_MODE_AND_DECREES.md` §13–§19 and `docs/ARCH_EDIT_MODE_AND_DECREES.md` read whole there. Nothing in the main checkout's source was read; nothing was committed; no npm. This seat MEASURES and rules nothing: every "CANDIDATE" below is a candidate, never a ruling.
**Instruments:** grep/sed over the worktree, and small read-only `node` probes that IMPORT the base's own modules (`_work-EM-FP-SEAM/*.mjs`; outputs `*.out`). `resolve-hook.mjs` resolves only BARE third-party specifiers (one: `seedrandom`) from the main checkout's `node_modules`; all project source is the base's.
**Labels:** CONFIRMED-EXEC = a probe executed the base's code; CONFIRMED-READ = the literal text / an exhaustive grep shows it; PLAUSIBLE = reasoning over read code, not executed.

## Outcome first

1. **No seal can open in a real world at the base.** Sixteen seals; ONE is bound (`acceptPeace` → `make-peace`) and its predicate `pendingPeaceOffer` has NO reachable `src/` writer (`peaceTermsDrafting.js :: withPeaceOffer` has zero `src/` callers). The fifteen others are SHUT. Independently, the shell reads §18 with `campaignState = null`, so every campaign-reading predicate is false at the mount, and a seal opens only when its condition names EXACTLY ONE subject — which only `pendingPeaceOffer` can. (CONFIRMED-EXEC + CONFIRMED-READ.)
2. **For eleven of the sixteen seals the §18 predicate IS satisfiable by live `src/` writers** (war layer, coup stressor, trade edges, belief maps, generation's roster); four are not (the two peace-offer seals, the two envoy seals); `scheduleEvent`'s `always` is not a predicate row at all. (CONFIRMED-EXEC on writer-shaped state; writer reachability CONFIRMED-READ.)
3. **The editor binds NONE of the 14 proposal-lane realm verbs.** `directions.js` declares `direct-realm` over exactly those 14 plus 11 more directions, but the leaf has zero `src/` importers and none of its 12 types is in `OP_TYPES` (22 rows). The realm verbs are directable TODAY only through the map's `RealmVerbComposer.jsx` (proposal lane). (CONFIRMED-EXEC + grep.)
4. **Registry: 86 rows, 6 with an outcome vocabulary.** 17 rows are FP-owned by the brief's families (only HBF-05 has a vocabulary); treaty/peace/oath/habit/epoch/WC/seat/caravan modules have ZERO rows AND take ZERO draws. The unregistered FP-side draws are all in `traditions/*` (9 symbols, 23 sites, invisible to the survey's idiom set), plus two keyed-hash choosers outside every idiom (`npcCirculationBelief.js :: rumourReinforcesAt`, `brokeragePatronage.js :: genesisPatronOf`). (CONFIRMED-EXEC scan.)
5. **Pin consult: two forks are consultable (HBF-85 traditions, HBF-86 siege); no `src/` module mints a `pin-fork`,** `pin-fork` is not in `OP_TYPES`, a pending pin is WITHDRAWN at the tick by the realm catalogue, and the bag is keyed by fork id alone (realm-wide per tick). HBF-05's pin seam is unfed. (CONFIRMED-EXEC; the realm-wide scope is PLAUSIBLE.)
6. **Roots: no FP record family is a root or a derived card with provenance;** `peaceOffers` alone is classed (HELD, saved-only, unwritten). (CONFIRMED-EXEC + READ.)
7. **Missions: `send-on-mission` is dark end to end;** standalone covert missions have no `src/` minter; the only spy path wired into the pulse is a rider on a diplomatic embassy, and every envoy/espionage flag it needs is lit by no `src/` site and no DM toggle. (CONFIRMED-READ.)

**Evidence and structural facts**
- CONFIRMED-EXEC (`conds.mjs`): with `campaignState = null` and a record carrying people, only `npcPresent` holds, with 0 subjects; every campaign row is false. On writer-shaped state (`war_front` + `hostile` edge, `deployments`, a live `coup_detat`, a confirmed `trade_route`, a `trade_partner` relationship state, a `beliefMaps` entry) `warInProgress`, `forceInField`, `siegeInProgress`, `plotInMotion`, `openRoute`, `tradeWith` and `beliefExists` all hold. `pendingPeaceOffer` holds, with subject `["s2"]`, only after the unreached `withPeaceOffer` is called by the probe itself.
- THE OPEN RULE (`EditModeShell.jsx :: sealRow`, CONFIRMED-READ): `named = bound && subjects.length === 1 ? subjects[0] : ''`. Nine of the ten condition rows declare no `subjects` (`worldConditions.js :: worldConditionSubjects` returns the shared empty list). So binding any other seal also needs a `subjects` reader on its row.
- THE MOUNT READS NO CAMPAIGN (CONFIRMED-READ): `EditModeShell.jsx` calls `phantomMintAction.js :: worldConditionsOf(settlement)`, which calls `worldConditionHolds(id, settlement, null)`. The shell's header says that is deliberate (`campaignRuntimeCallerCoverage` forbids arming the campaign runtime there). So seven campaign-reading conditions are false at the mount by construction, whatever the world holds.
- THE GATE (CONFIRMED-READ): `authSlice.js :: canEditSettlement` is `TIER_GATE[tier]?.settlementEditor === true && isStaffRole(role)`, so the register renders for staff only (U16's signed act lifts the second conjunct).
- THE BOUND ACT CHANGES NO WAR (CONFIRMED-READ; the end-to-end claim is PLAUSIBLE): `decreeHook.js :: applyDecreesAtTick` turns a due entry into a cause receipt and "invents no world state". The literal `'make-peace'` appears in `src/` only in `operationsOffStage.js` and `editSlice.js :: SEAL_ACTS`, so no tick consumer acts on the cause, and nothing calls `withoutPeaceOffer`.

**Does the editor bind ANY of them? NO** (CONFIRMED-READ + EXEC). EM-E5 authored the binding shape: `directions.js :: REALM_DIRECTION_VERBS` (executed: the 14), `realmSealsFor`, `realmSealReason` and `realmRefusalProse`, with `REALM_VETO_PROSE` as the herald's line. But `directions.js` has zero `src/` importers, `direct-realm` ∉ `OP_TYPES`, and no `SEALS` row names a verb. The header's own words: "The composition into one catalogue is the chair's, at the unfreeze."

**CANDIDATE seal → verb mapping (a measurement of fit, NOT a ruling)**

| seal | CANDIDATE verb | fit | the divergence a ruling must settle |
|---|---|---|---|
| `suePeace` | `SUE_FOR_PEACE` | direct | the seal's `warInProgress` (a front or a `hostile` rung) ≠ the verb's own predicate (a live un-recalled deployment). Ruling 2 says the VERB's predicate IS the §18 condition, so `SEALS.war[suePeace].needs` would move. The verb's dial `partyId` also names the suing court, which the card's subject does not fix |
| `embargo` | `DECLARE_TRADE_EMBARGO` | direct | the seal's `tradeWith` ≠ the verb's (supply-web lit ∧ ≥2 courts). HBF-18 `supplyWebWarfare.js :: declareTradeEmbargo` is registered, with no vocabulary |
| `letCoupFail` (and "succeed") | `ORDER_INTERVENTION`, `side` INCUMBENT / CHALLENGER | partial | the verb is a THIRD court backing a side, not the home letting a coup fail. Its condition (`liveCoupContests`) ≠ `plotInMotion`. The design's own pin (`resolveCoupVerdict`) is unregistered |
| `resupply` | `ORDER_CONVOY` | weak | naval only, and it needs a deployed army (`convoy_no_deployment`). The tree has no land resupply act |
| `scheduleEvent` | `FORCE_CALAMITY` (realm scale) | partial | the design binds `scheduleEvent` to the SETTLEMENT catalogue (`eventCatalogue.js`) |
| `letSiegeFall` | none (a pin on HBF-86); `ORDER_SUPPLY_RAID` is indirect | — | — |
| `directForce` | none in the manifest; `DIRECTION_OP_TYPES['direct-force']` is the tree's own | — | — |
| `acceptPeace` · `refusePeace` · `receiveEnvoy` · `turnEnvoyAway` · `directTrade` · the 3 rumour seals · `sendOnMission` | no realm verb | — | — |
| **verbs with no seal on any card** | `DECLARE_CASUS`, `REPUDIATE_TREATY`, `TRANSFER_SOVEREIGNTY`, `ORDER_SUPPLY_RAID`, `DECLARE_BLOCKADE`, `FORCE_RECONSIDERATION`, `FORCE_FOUND_STEADING`, `FORCE_ABANDON`, `FORCE_RESETTLE` (the last two owner-gated) | — | FP-relevant: `REPUDIATE_TREATY` has no treaty card to live on; `DECLARE_CASUS` belongs on the war card |

## 4. THE DECISION-FORK REGISTRY

**Where it is.** `src/domain/worldPulse/habitForkRegistry.js :: HABIT_FORK_REGISTRY` (`directives.js`' header names it as the pin target). The generator's twin is `src/domain/generation/generationForkRegistry.js :: GENERATION_TIER1 / GENERATION_TIER2`: generation roots only, with no FP family.
**Executed (`forks.mjs`, `forkkeys.mjs`):** **86 rows** (the survey counted 42 at `ed9d99295`; EM-E0 added HBF-43..86) · DEFER 77 / STAY 9 · **6 rows declare an outcome vocabulary** (`actionVocabulary`): HBF-05 `STAY_DETECTION_OUTCOMES`, HBF-17 `STRATEGY_MOVES`, HBF-69 `RETURN_ODDS_WORDS`, HBF-84 `STRESSOR_LIFECYCLE_STAGES`, HBF-85 `TRADITION_OUTCOME`, HBF-86 `SIEGE_VERDICT_BANDS` · 1 row has `symbol: null` (HBF-23 piety) · row keys: `forkId, module, symbol, discovery, disposition, arity, actionVocabulary, closeSource, closeOwed, domain, reason, circumstanceClasses`.

**FP-owned rows, by the brief's families (module-path match): 17.** Populations/migration: HBF-01, 02, 03, 12, 54, 68. Espionage: HBF-04, 05, 06, 31. Info/rumor/belief: HBF-33, 58, 71, 72, 73, 74. Faith/patron: HBF-34. **Only HBF-05 carries a vocabulary.**
**FP-adjacent (my classification, PLAUSIBLE): 16.** FAITH-domain: HBF-21, 22, 23, 67 and HBF-85 (with a vocabulary). Missions: HBF-35. Envoy/errand layer: HBF-36..44 and HBF-46.
**Zero rows for treaty\*, peace\*, oath\*, habit\*, epoch\*, the war-circulation modules, seat/court and caravan/wayfare.** Those modules also take ZERO draws: a grep of `treaty*.js`, `peace*.js`, `oathHolder.js`, `warPeace*.js`, `seatIntervention.js`, `warSeatBooks.js`, `rulingPowerSeat.js`, `contributionLedger.js`, `peopleLedger.js`, `warStance.js`, `advanceEpochLedger.js` and `habit/*.js` for `rng|random|hash01|createPRNG` finds only one comment line. - **Founding choosers:** `traditions/genesis.js :: deriveFoundingTraditions`, `buildWindow`, `composeName` and `pickTrappings`, and `traditions/customFounding.js :: adaptCustomTradition`.   - `worldPulse/brokeragePatronage.js :: genesisPatronOf`: `kernel/proseHash.js :: pickVariant`, i.e. `fnv1a32 % n`, choosing "the power a house was born serving" (patron family; a genesis binding, root-shaped).
  - Neither has a registry row, and neither is visible to a draw-idiom scan.

## 5. THE PIN CONSULT

| consult site (`path :: symbol`) | fork | vocabulary in the kernel's map? | how the pins reach it | consultable by `pin-fork` today | FP? |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `worldPulse/warSiegeVerdict.js :: resolveSiegeVerdict` (`chooseOrPinFork(forkPins, SIEGE_FORK.id, thunk)`) | HBF-86 `warSiegeVerdict.js :: SIEGE_FORK` (4 band words) | yes | `pulseKernel.js :: simulateCampaignWorldPulse` → `warDeployment.js :: evaluateWarLayer({ forkPins })` | **YES** (the consult is live) | no (war) |
| `worldPulse/traditionsKernel.js :: advanceLitTraditions` | HBF-85 `traditionsKernel.js :: TRADITION_FORK` (5 words; `cancelled` excluded) | yes | `simulateCampaignWorldPulse` → `factionDensityKernel.js :: advanceNpcGrowthWith…AndDensity(args)` → (the wrapper chain passes `args` whole) → `traditionsKernel.js :: advanceNpcGrowthWithFabricAndConsequenceAndLadderAndTraditions` → `advanceTraditions({ forkPins: args.forkPins })` | **YES** | FAITH-domain (adjacent) |
| `worldPulse/espionage/espionageGauntlet.js :: stayDetectionRoll` (`pinnedOutcome`) via `advanceEspionageGauntlet({ pinnedCatch })` | HBF-05 `espionageGauntlet.js :: STAY_DETECTION_FORK_ID` / `STAY_DETECTION_OUTCOMES` | **no** (executed: an HBF-05 pin is refused at the fold and the consult draws) | `envoyPulse.js` calls `advanceEspionageGauntlet({ worldState: state, tick, snapshot, regionalGraph })` with **no `pinnedCatch`** | **NO** — the seam is built and unfed | yes (espionage) |
| `generators/pipeline.js :: chooseOrPin` (EM-P0; consulted in `assembleSettlement`, `generatePopulation`, `generatePower`, `assembleInstitutions`, `economyReconciliation`, `narrativeGenerator`) | record-path keys (`name`, `npcs`, `relationships`, `factions`, `conflicts`, `institutions`, `catalogForTier`, `generationRepairs`, `powerIntent`, `powerStructure`) | n/a | the re-derivation lane (`dmLayer.js`) | generation pins, not pulse forks | no FP family |

- **THE SOURCE** (CONFIRMED-READ). `pulseKernel.js :: simulateCampaignWorldPulse` composes `forkPinsFor(saves.flatMap(save => dueEntriesAtTick(worldState, save?.settlement?.decrees)), { [TRADITION_FORK.id]: …, [SIEGE_FORK.id]: … })`. `directives.js :: forkPinsFor` keys the bag by `forkId` ALONE (`pins[pin.forkId] = pin.outcome`), and both consults pass the fork id alone. So one pin covers EVERY siege (every festival) resolved in the realm that tick, not only the card's subject. That realm-wide effect is PLAUSIBLE; it was not run through the pulse.
- **THE PRODUCER: none.** `'pin-fork'` / `PIN_FORK_TYPE` appear only in `directives.js` (grep), and `pin-fork` ∉ `OP_TYPES` (executed). CONFIRMED-EXEC (`pinprobe.mjs`): a hand-built pending `pin-fork` on HBF-86 enters the bag (`{"HBF-86":"costly_success"}`) and the consult returns it. The same entry, run through `decreeHook.js :: applyDecreesAtTick` with `{ opTypes: OP_TYPES }` (what `campaignAdvanceSession.js :: decreeCataloguesForSaves` composes whenever an entry is pending), is **WITHDRAWN** (`vocabulary-moved`, `op-type`, `pin-fork`) with 0 causes. The kernel folds the pins BEFORE `applyDecreesToSaves` on the same composed line, so a pin that did exist would be honoured AND withdrawn in one tick. That is PLAUSIBLE: the ordering was read, not run.
- **FP-owned forks consultable today: HBF-85 only** (FAITH-domain, adjacent). None in the strict brief families; HBF-05 is the only strict FP row with a vocabulary, and its seam is unfed.
- **Design §19 ruling 3's first-cut forks at the base** (grep of the registry). Registered: `candidateRoll` (HBF-60), `advanceNpcCorruption` (HBF-76), `seasonalSeverityFor` (HBF-80), `advanceLitTraditions` (HBF-85, with vocabulary), `resolveSiegeVerdict` (HBF-86, with vocabulary), and the meeting family (HBF-36..42, no vocabulary). **Not registered:** `rulingPowerCoup.js :: resolveCoupVerdict`, `readWarTerminations`, `VERDICTS`, `ADMISSION_OUTCOMES`, `ENVOY_ERRAND_STATES`, `foldNarrativeTempo`.

- **FP-adjacent ROOTS:** `powerSeat.holder` (the seat), `npc.status` (the `NpcStatus` pool: exile, jail), and `worldFact.stressors`.
- **Structural fact** (CONFIRMED-READ): the register covers the SETTLEMENT RECORD's top-level keys. Every FP ledger except `peaceOffers` lives on the CAMPAIGN (`worldState` / `spatialLedgers`), so §14's root/derived classification has no row to put them in.

## 8. ENTITY STATES + MISSIONS

**`NpcStatus` at the base** (`src/domain/entities/npcs.js :: NPC_STATUS_VALUES`; the typedef and the values are pinned equal): `active`, `dead`, `exiled`, `jailed`, `missing`, `removed`, `retired`. That is seven values, `jailed` added by EM-B1d. `npcs.js :: NPC_UNAVAILABLE_STATUSES` = `dead`, `exiled`, `jailed`, `removed`. There is **no away-on-mission value**: design §19 ruling 8 says the errand record carries the position. The institution-state pool (`operations.js`, `INSTITUTION_STATE_POOL`) is `active`, `destroyed`, `impaired`, `removed`, `ruined`, `vacant`.

| mission kind | vocabulary (`path :: symbol`) | cast by | resolved by (the FP layer) | reachable in a real world today? |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **diplomatic envoy** (`sue`, `self_parlay`; class `diplomatic`) | `envoyErrandVocabulary.js :: ENVOY_PURPOSES`, `ENVOY_PURPOSE_CLASSES` (6: commercial, covert, diplomatic, factional, personal, religious), `ENVOY_ERRAND_STATES` (7) | `envoyCasting.js :: envoyCandidate` (importance-descending), from `envoyDiplomacy.js` | the envoy errand layer (`envoyPulse.js`, `envoyErrand*.js`: parlay, interception, return) | **dark in every preset world.** `envoyErrandVocabulary.js :: ENVOY_REQUIRED_RULES` needs `envoyDiplomacyEnabled`, `routeLifecycleEnabled`, `warTerminationEnabled` and `npcConsequencesEnabled`. No `src/` site sets any of the four true (grep: 0 each), and none is a DM toggle (`SimulationRulesAxes.jsx :: ENGINE_WAVES` holds 9 other keys) |
| **covert rider** (a spy aboard an embassy; `purposeClass: 'covert'`) | `espionage/espionageMissions.js :: COVERT_MISSION_VOCABULARY` (products acquire/confirm/refute · demands certain/confirm/corroborate · faces covert/declared · taps beliefs/delta/performance · legRefs · maxStops 3) | `espionage/espionageRider.js :: covertRiderFor` (HBF-06; `hash01` cadence), from `envoyDiplomacy.js` | the espionage layer: `espionageGauntlet.js :: advanceEspionageGauntlet` / `stayDetectionRoll` (HBF-05; the capture is computed with `custodyWritten: false`), and `espionageProductStage.js :: advanceEspionageProducts` → `espionageProducts.js :: landEspionageProduct` | dark: `espionage/espionageGate.js :: espionageActive` needs `beliefsActive`, `errandSpineEnabled` and `espionageEnabled`, and no `src/` site lights the last two. - **CONFIRMED-EXEC** (the base's own modules imported and run; scripts and outputs in `_work-EM-FP-SEAM/`):
  - `seals.mjs`: the 16 seals, and `SEAL_ACTS` = 1 row.

  - `pools.mjs`: declarations, pools, and the register's `peaceOffers` rows.
- **CONFIRMED-READ** (literal text or an exhaustive `src/` grep):
  - Zero `src/` callers or importers of `withPeaceOffer`, `withoutPeaceOffer`, `draftPeaceOffer`, `directions.js`, `eventCatalogue.js`, `missionDispatcher.js` (and its four exports), `castCovertOperative` and `mintCovertMission`.
  - `'pin-fork'` and `'make-peace'` literal sites.
  - Zero `src/` sites set `envoyDiplomacyEnabled`, `routeLifecycleEnabled`, `espionageEnabled`, `errandSpineEnabled`, `missionDispatcherEnabled`, `infoStatecraftEnabled`, `warTerminationEnabled` or `npcConsequencesEnabled` true.
  - `envoyPulse.js` passes no `pinnedCatch`.
  - `worldConditionsOf` passes `null`.
  - `canEditSettlement` is staff-only.
- **PLAUSIBLE** (reasoning, not executed):
  - A pin's realm-wide scope within a tick.
  - A pin honoured and its entry withdrawn in the same tick.
  - The claim that a bound `make-peace` would change no war state end to end.
  - The FP-adjacent classifications in §4 and §7.
  - That the war-layer, coup and trade writers fire in a real campaign on the presets named. The writers and gates were read; no pulse was run.
- **Reproduce:** `cd fp-kit/findings/_work-EM-FP-SEAM && node --import ./resolve-hook.mjs <probe>.mjs`. The hook resolves only bare third-party specifiers (`seedrandom`) from the main checkout's `node_modules`.

# FP WAVE CENSUS — family SP · CW · GR · IN · TR (measurement seat, read-only)

**Base:** `8b5565922` (train `em-train-17-2026-09-23`), read from the `fp-read` worktree and `git show` / `git grep` at that sha only. **Scope:** every §5 wave block of `docs/DESIGN_FP_ARCHITECTURE.md` whose id starts SP-, CW-, GR-, IN- or TR- — 34 blocks (#1–#31, #58–#60). SP-F has no §5 block (declared NON-WAVE); it landed at `1d3cdf736` and is not counted.

**Disposition rule used.** A wave is LANDED when every slice its packets and commits define has landed and every §5 charter item is present at the base. It is PARTIAL when some have, and the row names what remains. It is NOT STARTED when there is no landing commit, no packet and no charter module or flag gate at the base; contract pre-declarations, pre-registrations and seam comments are not landings. Deferrals that a landing assigned by name to a *different* wave are noted but do not demote the row.

**Clause columns.** The §5 block's own clause is quoted when it has one. Otherwise the clause is quoted verbatim from the per-program volume `docs/DESIGN_FP_ARCH_{SP,GR,IN,TR,CW}.md` at the base, which §5 names as normative where it compresses them, and is labelled `ARCH_xx`.

## Premises of the brief, the charter and §5 itself that the base refutes

1. **The flag list's path.** `ENGINE_GATED_VIRTUAL_RULE_KEYS` lives in `src/domain/worldPulse/simulationRules.js`, not `src/domain/simulationRules.js`. It holds 35 keys. Of the 23 flags this family charters (§3 rows 1-22 and 43), 11 are in it and 12 are absent. The seven TR-2..TR-8 flags appear only in `tradeConvergenceContract.js`.
2. **"Edit-verb and Alignment clauses exist in most blocks" is false for this family.** Only six of the 34 §5 blocks carry either clause: SP-A, CW-0w, GR-2, IN-2, IN-3 and TR-6 (TR-6's is its "ALIGNMENT NEVER GATES" sentence). Every other clause here comes from the per-program volumes. GR-7 has no clause in either place.
3. **The §5 PROGRESS block (re-derived 2026-08-09 at `2c810d16`) is stale in both directions.** GR-3b and IN-0c have since landed, and GR-4a..4e, GR-5A, IN-1a/1b/1c-a have landed too. Its "GR-0/1/2 LANDED" overstates GR-1, which has an outstanding charter item (the generosity stamp), and GR-2, whose `PROPOSE_PACT` verb was deferred to a GR-2b that never landed.
4. **Hot files at the base,** measured as eslint `max-lines` effective counts (skip blank lines and comments). The counter reproduced all seven `scripts/.size-baseline.json` values exactly. `peaceTerms.js` is 797/800. `generosityKernel.js` is 800/800 with no baseline entry. `beliefMap.js` is 781, `informationStatecraft.js` 782 and `realmItemReadModel.js` 784. `settlementStrategy.js` is 760 and `envoyErrand.js` is 714; neither is baselined. So the charters' "at ceiling" or "812 baseline" claims for `envoyErrand`, `settlementStrategy` and `realmItemReadModel` are refuted.

## Evidence classes (CONFIRMED vs PLAUSIBLE)

- **CONFIRMED (executed at the base):**
  - Commit presence and ancestry. `git log 8b5565922` was grepped for every id and spelling. Every cited sha was checked with `git merge-base --is-ancestor`.
  - Module and export presence or absence (`git ls-tree`, `git grep`).
  - Flag membership (the 35-key list was parsed).
  - Packet status, plus every CREATE path and every requiredSymbol of the 15 family packets. All 15 are present at the base.
  - The absence greps behind every "remaining" slice: `honored_by_silence`, a `repudiated` kind, `mirror_shift`, `PROPOSE_PACT` in the operation registry, the five route kinds' Herald rows, and `suspicionOf`.
- **PLAUSIBLE (reasoned or document-sourced):**
  - Which slices a packet *defines*. This is read from packet and commit prose.
  - SP-B2's lighting condition reading SATISFIED. This is inferred from the presence of the three evidence addresses; the walker was not run.
  - Effective-line counts for files outside the size baseline. These come from the validated approximator, not eslint itself.
  - Whether landed code is green. No test or gate was run (the brief forbids npm).

Work files (log dumps, scans, the line counter): `findings/_work-SP-CW-GR-IN-TR/`.

### PART E · family WF-POP-INT-WY

# FP WAVE CENSUS — family WF · POP · INT · WY-engine (+ the LG volume)

- **Base:** `8b5565922` (train `em-train-17-2026-09-23`), read-only worktree `<SC>/fp-read` (HEAD verified = `8b55659223246e638448c2cf80ecec4d6a9bea48`). Nothing in the main checkout was read. Measured 2026-09-23, 22:3x–22:5x EDT, by the WF/POP/INT/WY measurement seat.
- **Source of the blocks:** `git show 8b5565922:docs/DESIGN_FP_ARCHITECTURE.md`, section 5, blocks #32–#57 plus WY-1, WY-2, WY-3, WY-4, WY-5, WY-6 and WY-11, each read whole. The per-program volumes at the base (`docs/DESIGN_FP_ARCH_{WF,POP,INT,WY}.md`) were read for their per-wave specs.
- **Method (every row):** (a) all 5,692 commits reachable from the base were dumped (subjects and bodies) and grepped for `(WF|POP|INT|WY|LG)-<n>`; each hit's body was read to separate landings from design passes. (b) `docs/implementation/PACKET_MANIFEST.json`: the only family rows are WF-1A, WF-1B, WF-1C, WF-1D, WF-1E, WF-1F, WF-8A and INT-3B, and all eight are `LANDED`; there are no POP, WY or LG rows. (c) Each charter-named module was checked against `git ls-files`, and each symbol with `git grep` over src/, tests/ and scripts/. (d) Flags: `ENGINE_GATED_VIRTUAL_RULE_KEYS` and `ENGINE_GATED_DORMANT_RULE_KEYS` (35 members each, read by importing the module at the base), `DEFAULT_SIMULATION_RULES`, and every preset's `.rules`.
